== 0. Removing effects: each effect loaded, played, its window opened and removed 8 times, then 6 copies removed at once (each effect in its own process) ==
22 of 22 effects were removed and deleted 14 times each without a problem

== 1. Big project: 10 bands x 20 slots ==
Loaded 200 effects in 3772.16 ms; Triad latency 1166 samples
10 s of audio through 200 effects: average 13.67 ms per 256-sample block (256.2% of one core), worst 26.44 ms, peak level -33.6 dBFS
Switched on every LFO: 220 knobs now move with the song (slot mixes, band mixes, band volumes)
10 s of audio through 200 effects with every LFO on: average 14.16 ms per 256-sample block (265.5% of one core), worst 27.22 ms, peak level -15.9 dBFS
LFO cost: 3.6% more processing than the same project without LFOs
Saved (4178 KB) in 133.88 ms, reopened in 4267.13 ms with 200 effects running
Undo with 200 effects: a snapshot takes 181.07 ms; undoing a removed effect 108.29 ms (it is loaded again, the others stay as they are); undoing a slot mix 105.49 ms; redoing both 210.58 ms
Closed the 200-effect project in 5.73 ms

(Seed 777 in its own process, like one DAW session: 21 effects, each plug-in in one format, VST3 where it has both)

(For comparison: a singer 1 m from the microphone arrives 2.9 ms late; a 64-sample buffer is 1.33 ms.)
18 of 22 effects add no latency; these do (Triad then reports the largest band total to the DAW): Graillon 3 (VST3, Auburn Sounds) 1074 samples (22.38 ms); BYOD (VST3, chowdsp) 4 samples (0.08 ms); CHOWTapeModel (VST3, chowdsp) 39 samples (0.81 ms); FILT-R (VST3, Tilr) 49 samples (1.02 ms)
With Graillon 3 (VST3, Auburn Sounds) in band 2 (slot at 0% mix): Triad reports 1074 samples (22.38 ms); the dry sound comes out at sample 1074 (peak at 1075)
Vocal chain effects: ChowMultiTool (VST3, chowdsp), ChowCentaur (VST3, chowdsp), TIME-12 (VST3, Tilr), Dragonfly Plate Reverb (VST3, Michael Willis), Dragonfly Room Reverb (VST3, Michael Willis)
Each case plays 8 s per buffer size at 48 kHz on an audio thread paced like an interface, with Triad's window open. Times are Triad's own work per block (CPU time); "late": the work took longer than the buffer lasts (a dropout on any machine); "paused by the system": the test machine (shared, virtual) stopped the thread for a moment, not Triad's work; "silenced": blocks Triad output as silence because its window was changing the bands at that moment. The project is saved every second meanwhile, like a DAW's autosave.

== 7. Recording through Triad: the extra-strong live test ==
The session: Graillon 3 (VST3, Auburn Sounds) in PRE; 3 bands at 250 Hz / 2.5 kHz (24 dB/oct) with ChowMultiTool (VST3, chowdsp), ChowCentaur (VST3, chowdsp), TIME-12 (VST3, Tilr), Dragonfly Plate Reverb (VST3, Michael Willis), Dragonfly Room Reverb (VST3, Michael Willis); every LFO on (band volumes as a 60% tremolo) and an LFO linked to a knob inside an effect; Triad's window open; the project saved every second.
(a) Monitoring while you work: a knob turned, an LFO drawn, a window opened, an undo step kept... every 150 ms. "Silenced": blocks Triad output as silence because its window was changing the bands at that moment; "dropouts": output blocks that were silent while the voice was there, for any reason; "late": Triad's work took longer than the buffer lasts.

== 8. Pitch correction with a sung vocal: a plain track, Triad's PRE, and a copy in each band (250 Hz / 2.5 kHz) ==
